- **fashionmaxxin**: PostToolUse, PreCompact, PreToolUse, SessionEnd, SessionStart, Stop, UserPromptSubmit on *, Bash, Edit|Write|NotebookEdit, Read. Kailash COC Claude (Rust) — Claude Code hooks + permissions. Trust-posture state-file writes are guarded by hooks (validate-bash-command.js detectStateFileMutation for Bash + posture-gate/integrity-guard for Edit/Write), NOT a Bash deny-matrix.
